L16: ### Node Move
L17: 
L18: The Node Move feature enables you to select and then manually move a specific node on the mesh to improve the local mesh quality.
L19: #### Requirements
L20: 
L21: This feature has the following topological requirements:
L22: 
L23:   * Node movement is restricted to the target part. That is, nodes cannot be moved outside of the part.
L24: 
L25:   * Face/Edge nodes can be moved along the corresponding face/edge only.
L26: 
L27:   * The application locks corner nodes to the underlying vertex.
L28:   * Moving a node on a solved analysis causes the solution data to become obsolete. However, the state does not become obsolete for other objects that depend on the mesh (for example, imported loading conditions, Element Orientations). If your node movement changes affect other objects, you will need to manually clear and refresh these objects to apply the new changes.
L29: 
L30:   * The Node Move feature is disabled if you are working with cite5†Section Planes .
L31: #### Application
L32: 
L33: The Node Move object is a child object of cite6†Mesh Edit . It is inserted into the tree by selecting the Node Move button on the cite7†Mesh Edit toolbar or by select<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1593" ## Tables

### New Table Variables

New independent and dependent variables are available in the Tables function to support Mechanical APDL scripts that contain the *DIM command. These new variables include field variables and dimensionless numeric data.

Back to Top

## Mesh

### Mesh Workflows

The Mesh Workflows feature has been enhanced to:

  * FSI FEM Acoustics

Use the FSI FEM Acoustics workflow to define acoustic domains for meshing a multi-body parts in a fluid–structure interaction (FSI) analysis. This workflow includes a predefined template with Steps and Outcomes that guide you through creating and validating acoustic meshes for FSI simulations.

Image

  * Direct Morphing: This workflow includes a Direct Morph operation that provides multiple morphing controls to change the mesh by scaling, rotating, translating, or off-setting of edge or face entities. Morphing changes defined in a Mesh Workflow can be applied to the original mesh in-place using a new Mesh Edit control named Morph Control.

A hybrid strategy with structured critical regions and controlled unstructured transitions is acceptable when it produces better quality and a more robust model.\r\n\r\n## Fluent and microchannel adaptation\r\n\r\n- Keep full 3D fluid and solid volumes for conjugate heat transfer. Do not replace them with midsurfaces; midsurfaces are primarily a structural-shell simplification.\r\n- Split inlet/outlet plenums at their junctions with the active microchannel region when this creates sweepable blocks.\r\n- Align solid partitions with fluid partitions at coupled interfaces where practical.\r\n- Preserve one continuous fluid path and verify that internal cuts become conformal `interior` or coupled interfaces, never accidental walls.\r\n- Avoid cuts through tooth tips, very small fillets, and narrow gaps unless the cut removes a known meshing obstruction.\r\n\r\n## Execute and audit\r\n\r\n1. Work on a new versioned copy; never overwrite the last validated geometry.\r\n2. Record body count, volume, units, and physical boundary map before cutting.\r\n3. Create the minimum useful cuts, then form parts/share topology as required for a conformal mesh.\r\n4. Rebuild Named Selections by geometric meaning rather than relying on old face IDs.\r\n5. Recheck body count, total fluid/solid volumes, gaps, overlaps, and non-manifold edges.\r\n6. Show the confirmed partition map once more before mesh generation if the executed topology differs materially from the proposal.\r\n7. Generate the mesh and record cell/node counts, minimum orthogonal quality, maximum skewness or aspect ratio, invalid-cell count, and the location of the worst cells.\r\n8. Compare against the uncut or previous version. Keep the partitioned model only when it improves meshability or quality without changing the intended physics.\r\n\r\nPartitioning does not authorize changing boundary conditions, materials, solver models, or the physical geometry dimensions.\r\n"}

Transforms (https://ansyshelp.ansys.com/public/Views/Secured/corp/v242/en/mech_obj/ds_geometry_transforms_o_r.html)
citeturn37search0 [wordlim: 200] Published: 2.2 years ago; Controls whether the application automatically transforms the mesh of your transformed parts. ...   * Right-click the Model object or in the Geometry window and select Insert > Part Transform.

## Transforms

Houses all of the part transformations that you create.

## Object Properties

The Details Pane properties for this object include the following.

Category  | Properties/Options/Descriptions
--- | ---
Definition  | Transform Mesh: Options include Yes (default) and No. Controls whether the application automatically transforms the mesh of your transformed parts. Setting this property to No instructs the application to clear the mesh of your transformed parts when updated. Regenerate Contacts: Options include Yes and No (default). This property controls whether the application automatically generates contact on your transformed parts.

## Tree Dependencies

  * Valid Parent Tree Object: Model.

## Insertion Methods

  * Select the Model object and then select the Part Transform option on the Model Context Tab.

  * Right-click the Model object or in the Geometry window and select Insert > Part Transform.

## Right-click Options

In addition to common right-click options, relevant right-click options for this object include:

  * Insert > Part Transform

  * Show All Bodies

  * Invert Visibility

  * Transform

  * Suppress/Unsuppress

## API Reference

See the Part Transform Group section of the ACT API Reference Guide for specific scripting information.

## Additional Related Information

Specifying Part Transformations.--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Reference supporting files only when they are relevant.]\n+# ANSYS 歧管微通道网格质量\n+\n+优先复用已验证的方法和脚本，按当前模型重新验收。当前有 **2 个完整成功案例**，另有 **50 juchi 未解决案例**；不能称三个均已解决，也不能保证任意结构必定达标。\n+\n+开始时读取 [案例库](references/cases.json) 和 [实际经验](references/experience.md)。执行时按需读取 [流程与工具](references/workflow.md)；遇到软件或接口问题再查 [排错记录](references/troubleshooting.md)。\n+\n+## 本地约定与范围\n+\n+- 保留 Workbench → ANSYS Meshing → Fluent 链路；不要自行改用 Fluent Meshing 或开始物理求解。\n+- 以用户当前保存的 `.wbpj` 和完整 `_files` 为准。heat 改动后，旧几何的成功结果不能沿用。目录下只有一个 `.wbpj` 时可自行解析，无需反复询问文件名。\n+- 默认目标是 **最低正交质量严格大于 0.07**；当前任务另有要求时采用当前目标，勿把 0.07 误写成 0.7。\n+- 试验和归档优先放 D 盘。先在独立副本中工作。仅当用户已授权覆盖时替换原工程，且先完整备份工程与 `_files`；不重复<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2473" />用。\n+- 网格尺寸增长属性为 `GrowthRateSF`；`GrowthRate` 可能属于膨胀层。`SmoothingIterations` 是膨胀层后的平滑，不能当作 PI 体网格的通用优化次数。\n+- 局部 proximity 的有效控件为 `UseProximitySize`。PCTetAutomaticNodeMovement 关闭后，PCTetFilletCollapse 可能变为只读，不要继续给只读依赖项赋值。\n+- 失败消息读取 `ExtAPI.Application.Messages` 中的 **DisplayString**、Severity、RelatedObjects，不是不存在的 Message 属性。\n+- Meshing 指标、导出文件、保存完成必须有新鲜哨兵；SendCommand 的 Python 失败不一定可靠地变成 Workbench 返回码。不能只看进程退出。\n+- Fluent 日志/输出文件每次使用新目录和名称；覆盖提示会打断批处理。验收需要完整的 Mesh Check、质量报告和正常 Transcript Stop，无 Error/负体积/左手单元。\n+- HDF5 的 `faces/c1` 是按全局 face ID 存的稀疏块，按 minId/maxId 填入，不能直接拼接。wall/shadow 的 c1 区域元数据不等同于第二侧真实体积贡献；只对 true interior 同时计算 c0/c1。\n+- Fluent 可以复制耦合壁面节点。按坐标分组合并处理同位置副本，不能只移动一侧。Fluent 重导出会重新编号，跨 case 比较不能直接把节点 ID 当同一个点。\n+- 四面体质量预测不是验收；金字塔、六面体等必须另外约束或固定相关节点。默认成功算法仅验证了入口/板的选择，全区域优化选项属于待验证扩展。\n+- 边界审计预读连接数组，避免每个边界区域重新解压同一个大 HDF5 数据块。对本地大 chunk 数据集直接整块写坐标曾发生 h5py 访问冲突；若确需原型写入，重建小 chunk 的副本，且之后仍以原生导出作为验收输入。\n+- 修改 heat 后，旧 STEP 可能落后于工程内 Geom.scdoc/Geom.agdb。不要为了得到旧网格而回退到切割前几何。\n*** End Patch"));
